  answer_t: x + {{q}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Simplify the following algebraic fraction:</p><p style="font-size:1.15em;text-align:center;margin:16px 0;"><span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">x² + 6x + 8</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">x + 4</span></span></p>"  → A="x + 2"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Simplify the following algebraic fraction:</p><p style="font-size:1.15em;text-align:center;margin:16px 0;"><span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">x² + 8x + 7</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">x + 1</span></span></p>"  → A="x + 7"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Simplify the following algebraic fraction:</p><p style="font-size:1.15em;text-align:center;margin:16px 0;"><span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">x² + 7x + 6</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">x + 1</span></span></p>"  → A="x + 6"
   trap 1: x + {{p}}
   trap 2: {{q}}
   expl_t: Factorise the numerator: x² + {{p+q}}x + {{p*q}} = (x + {{p}})(x + {{q}})<br>Cancel the common factor (x + {{p}}):<br>{{frac("(x + " + p + ")(x + " + q + ")", "x + " + p)}} = <strong>x + {{q}}</strong>

— Completing the Square  [LIVE]  diff 4  type:exact  tol:—
  id: 48d6b11f-ad99-4ed7-8ca4-4e505767022f
  template: <p>Write <strong>x² + {{2 * a}}x + {{b}}</strong> in the form (x + p)² + q.</p>
  answer_t: {{b - a * a >= 0 ? "(x + " + a + ")² + " + (b - a * a) : "(x + " + a + ")² − " + Math.abs(b - a * a)}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Write <strong>x² + 2x + 16</strong> in the form (x + p)² + q.</p>"  → A="(x + 1)² + 15"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Write <strong>x² + 12x + 15</strong> in the form (x + p)² + q.</p>"  → A="(x + 6)² − 21"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Write <strong>x² + 8x + 14</strong> in the form (x + p)² + q.</p>"  → A="(x + 4)² − 2"
   trap 1: (x + {{2 * a}})² + {{b}}
   trap 2: (x + {{a}})² + {{b + a * a}}
   expl_t: Halve the x coefficient: (x + {{a}})² = x² + {{2*a}}x + {{a*a}}. <br>Then adjust the constant: x² + {{2*a}}x + {{b}} = {{b - a * a >= 0 ? "(x + " + a + ")² + " + (b - a * a) : "(x + " + a + ")² − " + Math.abs(b - a * a)}}

— Composite Functions  [LIVE]  diff 3  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: 53d8b86c-c090-4085-bce2-82926c3ac26d
  template: <p>f(x) = {{a}}x + {{b}} and g(x) = x²</p><p>Find <strong>fg({{n}})</strong>.</p>
  answer_t: {{a*n*n + b}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>f(x) = 3x + 4 and g(x) = x²</p><p>Find <strong>fg(3)</strong>.</p>"  → A="31"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>f(x) = 2x + 5 and g(x) = x²</p><p>Find <strong>fg(2)</strong>.</p>"  → A="13"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>f(x) = 2x + 6 and g(x) = x²</p><p>Find <strong>fg(4)</strong>.</p>"  → A="38"
   trap 1: {{(a*n+b)*(a*n+b)}}
   trap 2: {{a*n + b}}
   trap 3: {{n*n}}
   expl_t: fg means "apply g first, then f".<br>Step 1: g({{n}}) = {{n}}² = {{n*n}}<br>Step 2: f({{n*n}}) = {{a}} × {{n*n}} + {{b}} = <strong>{{a*n*n + b}}</strong>

— Difference of Two Squares  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:expression  tol:—
  id: e291d373-5e2b-4bfd-be16-7d5efec5e849
  template: <p>Factorise <strong>x² − {{a * a}}</strong></p>
  answer_t: (x + {{a}})(x − {{a}})
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Factorise <strong>x² − 36</strong></p>"  → A="(x + 6)(x − 6)"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Factorise <strong>x² − 9</strong></p>"  → A="(x + 3)(x − 3)"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Factorise <strong>x² − 25</strong></p>"  → A="(x + 5)(x − 5)"
   trap 1: (x − {{a}})(x − {{a}})
   trap 2: (x - {{a}})²
   trap 3: (x + {{a}})(x + {{a}})
   expl_t: Difference of two squares: x² − {{a*a}} = x² − {{a}}² = (x + {{a}})(x − {{a}})

— Equation of a Circle  [draft]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: 4626a9a2-a69d-4e14-bce5-2c07751132c9
  template: <p>A circle has equation:</p><p style="font-size:1.15em;text-align:center;margin:16px 0;">x² + y² = {{r*r}}</p><p>Find the <strong>radius</strong> of the circle.</p>
  answer_t: {{r}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>A circle has equation:</p><p style="font-size:1.15em;text-align:center;margin:16px 0;">x² + y² = 16</p><p>Find the <strong>radius</strong> of the circle.</p>"  → A="4"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>A circle has equation:</p><p style="font-size:1.15em;text-align:center;margin:16px 0;">x² + y² = 9</p><p>Find the <strong>radius</strong> of the circle.</p>"  → A="3"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>A circle has equation:</p><p style="font-size:1.15em;text-align:center;margin:16px 0;">x² + y² = 64</p><p>Find the <strong>radius</strong> of the circle.</p>"  → A="8"
   trap 1: {{r*r}}
   trap 2: {{2*r}}
   expl_t: The equation x² + y² = r² represents a circle centred at the origin with radius r.<br>Here r² = {{r*r}}, so the radius = √{{r*r}} = <strong>{{r}}</strong>.

— Expanding Brackets  [LIVE]  diff 1  type:expression  tol:—
  id: e535a144-0b5b-4ae2-b80c-3701eae9f595
  template: <p>Expand <strong>{{a}}({{b}}x + {{c}})</strong></p>
  answer_t: {{a * b}}x + {{a * c}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Expand <strong>7(5x + 2)</strong></p>"  → A="35x + 14"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Expand <strong>9(9x + 5)</strong></p>"  → A="81x + 45"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Expand <strong>4(6x + 8)</strong></p>"  → A="24x + 32"
   trap 1: {{a}}{{b}}x + {{a}}{{c}}
   trap 2: {{a * b}}x + {{c}}
   expl_t: {{a}} × {{b}}x = {{a * b}}x and {{a}} × {{c}} = {{a * c}}, so the answer is {{a * b}}x + {{a * c}}

— Expanding Double Brackets  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:exact  tol:—
  id: dfa2e8a0-67e7-4847-b686-7cd008a1c778
  template: <p>Expand and simplify <strong>(x + {{a}})(x − {{b}})</strong></p>
  answer_t: {{a > b ? "x^2+" + (a-b) + "x-" + (a*b) : "x^2-" + (b-a) + "x-" + (a*b)}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Expand and simplify <strong>(x + 8)(x − 6)</strong></p>"  → A="x^2+2x-48"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Expand and simplify <strong>(x + 7)(x − 1)</strong></p>"  → A="x^2+6x-7"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Expand and simplify <strong>(x + 2)(x − 5)</strong></p>"  → A="x^2-3x-10"
   trap 1: {{a > b ? "x^2+" + (a+b) + "x-" + (a*b) : "x^2+" + (b-a) + "x-" + (a*b)}}
   trap 2: {{a > b ? "x^2+" + (a-b) + "x+" + (a*b) : "x^2-" + (b-a) + "x+" + (a*b)}}
   trap 3: x^2+{{a}}x-{{b}}x+{{a*b}}
   trap 4: x^2-{{b}}x+{{a}}x+{{a*b}}
   expl_t: FOIL: x² − {{b}}x + {{a}}x − {{a * b}} = x² + {{a - b}}x − {{a * b}}

— Expanding Double Brackets  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:exact  tol:—
  id: 4845d775-2463-4a9b-b04e-42abe64c4215
  template: <p>Expand and simplify <strong>(x + {{a}})(x + {{b}})</strong></p>
  answer_t: x² + {{a + b}}x + {{a * b}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Expand and simplify <strong>(x + 3)(x + 9)</strong></p>"  → A="x² + 12x + 27"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Expand and simplify <strong>(x + 9)(x + 7)</strong></p>"  → A="x² + 16x + 63"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Expand and simplify <strong>(x + 7)(x + 9)</strong></p>"  → A="x² + 16x + 63"
   trap 1: x² + {{a * b}}
   trap 2: x² + {{a}}x + {{b}}x + {{a * b}}
   trap 3: x² + {{b}}x + {{a}}x + {{a * b}}
   expl_t: FOIL: x × x = x², x × {{b}} = {{b}}x, {{a}} × x = {{a}}x, {{a}} × {{b}} = {{a * b}}. Collect: x² + {{a + b}}x + {{a * b}}

— Factorising  [LIVE]  diff 1  type:exact  tol:—
  id: 7ac8474b-3108-436a-a995-2668482ff8b8
  template: <p>Factorise fully <strong>{{a * b}}x + {{a * c}}</strong></p>
  answer_t: {{a}}({{b}}x + {{c}})
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Factorise fully <strong>18x + 30</strong></p>"  → A="6(3x + 5)"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Factorise fully <strong>18x + 12</strong></p>"  → A="6(3x + 2)"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Factorise fully <strong>8x + 12</strong></p>"  → A="4(2x + 3)"
   trap 1: ({{a * b}}x + {{a * c}})
   trap 2: {{a}}({{a * b}}x + {{a * c}})
   trap 3: 2({{a * b / 2}}x + {{a * c / 2}})
   expl_t: The HCF (highest common factor) of {{a * b}} and {{a * c}} is {{a}}. Divide both terms by {{a}}: {{a}}({{b}}x + {{c}})

— Factorising Quadratics  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:expression  tol:—
  id: 5582125e-cfaa-4e4b-8dd9-421c735700c3
  template: <p>Factorise <strong>x² + {{a + b}}x + {{a * b}}</strong></p>
  answer_t: (x + {{a}})(x + {{b}})
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Factorise <strong>x² + 10x + 21</strong></p>"  → A="(x + 7)(x + 3)"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Factorise <strong>x² + 7x + 10</strong></p>"  → A="(x + 2)(x + 5)"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Factorise <strong>x² + 14x + 48</strong></p>"  → A="(x + 8)(x + 6)"
   trap 1: (x + {{a * b}})(x + 1)
   trap 2: (x + {{a + b}})(x + 1)
   expl_t: Find two numbers that multiply to {{a * b}} and add to {{a + b}}. Those are {{a}} and {{b}}, so the answer is (x + {{a}})(x + {{b}})

— Finding the nth Term  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:expression  tol:—
  id: 26e0949b-c952-4e9e-9134-db6a6cb6a99e
  template: <p>Find the nth term of the sequence: <strong>{{a + b}}, {{a + 2*b}}, {{a + 3*b}}, {{a + 4*b}}, ...</strong></p>
  answer_t: {{b}}n + {{a}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Find the nth term of the sequence: <strong>8, 13, 18, 23, ...</strong></p>"  → A="5n + 3"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Find the nth term of the sequence: <strong>-6, -5, -4, -3, ...</strong></p>"  → A="n - 7"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Find the nth term of the sequence: <strong>16, 24, 32, 40, ...</strong></p>"  → A="8n + 8"
   trap 1: {{b}}n
   trap 2: {{b}}n + {{a + b}}
   expl_t: Common difference = {{b}}, so nth term starts with {{b}}n. <br>When n=1: {{b}} × 1 = {{b}}. However, the first term is {{a + b}}, so you still need to add {{a}}. <br>The nth term = {{b}}n + {{a}}.

   expl_t: Quadratic formula: x = {{frac('-b ± √(b²−4ac)', '2a')}}<br>Here a = 1, b = −{{p}}, c = −{{q}}.<br>Discriminant = (−{{p}})² − 4(1)(−{{q}}) = {{p*p}} + {{4*q}} = {{p*p + 4*q}}<br>x = {{frac(p + ' ± √' + (p*p + 4*q), 2)}}<br>Positive root: {{frac(p + ' + ' + round(Math.sqrt(p*p + 4*q), 4), 2)}} ≈ <strong>{{round((p + Math.sqrt(p*p + 4*q)) / 2, 2)}}</strong>

  id: 0d0af2a3-c4ec-4b2d-8877-80daa7dec768
  template: <p>Write the mixed number <strong>{{w}} {{frac(p, q)}}</strong> as an improper fraction.</p>

   · sample 1: Q="<p>Write the mixed number <strong>4 <span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">6</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">5</span></span></strong> as an improper fraction.</p>"  → A="26/5"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Write the mixed number <strong>4 <span style="display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.3;"><span style="border-bottom:1px solid currentColor;padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">2</span><span style="padding:0 4px;text-align:center;">7</span></span></strong> as an improper fraction.</p>"  → A="30/7"

   trap 1: {{w + p}}/{{q}}
   trap 2: {{w*p}}/{{q}}
   expl_t: Multiply the whole number by the denominator, then add the numerator:<br>{{w}} × {{q}} + {{p}} = {{w*q}} + {{p}} = {{w*q + p}}<br>Answer: {{frac(w*q + p, q)}}

   trap 4: {{a+1}}

— Standard Form  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:exact  tol:—

  template: <p>Write <strong>{{a}}000</strong> in standard form.</p>
  answer_t: {{Math.round(a * 1000 / Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(a * 1000))) * 10) / 10}} × 10<sup>{{Math.floor(Math.log10(a * 1000))}}</sup>

   · sample 2: Q="<p>Write <strong>2000</strong> in standard form.</p>"  → A="2 × 10<sup>3</sup>"

   trap 2: {{Math.round(a * 1000 / Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(a * 1000)) - 1) * 10) / 10}} × 10<sup>{{Math.floor(Math.log10(a * 1000)) - 1}}</sup>

   expl_t: Difference of two squares: ({{a}} + √{{b}})({{a}} − √{{b}}) = {{a}}² − (√{{b}})² = {{a * a}} − {{b}} = {{a * a - b}}

  id: 66cdd6fc-c0d2-4f0e-b567-b46e4a440b24
  template: <p>A fair spinner has <strong>{{a}} equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to {{a}}.</p><p>A second fair spinner has <strong>{{b}} equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to {{b}}.</p><p>Both spinners are spun once.</p><p>Find the probability of getting a <strong>1 on both spinners</strong>.    · sample 2: Q="<p>A fair spinner has <strong>8 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 8.</p><p>A second fair spinner has <strong>6 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 6.</p><p>Both spinners are spun once.</p><p>Find the probability of getting a <strong>1 on both spinners</strong>.    · sample 3: Q="<p>A fair spinner has <strong>5 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 5.</p><p>A second fair spinner has <strong>3 equal sections</strong> numbered 1 to 3.</p><p>Both spinners are spun once.</p><p>Find the probability of getting a <strong>1 on both spinners</strong>.    trap 1: {{a+b}}/{{a*b}}
   trap 2: 1/{{a}}
   trap 3: 1/{{b}}
   expl_t: The two spinners are independent, so multiply their probabilities:<br>P(1 on first spinner) = {{frac(1, a)}}<br>P(1 on second spinner) = {{frac(1, b)}}<br>P(1 on both) = {{frac(1, a)}} × {{frac(1, b)}} = {{frac(1, a*b)}}

— Conditional Probability  [draft]  diff 4  type:fraction  tol:—
  id: 6ed2d883-7e79-4c79-ad35-1a462b4a5ab5
  template: <p>A bag contains <strong>{{r}} red</strong> balls and <strong>{{b}} blue</strong> balls.</p><p>Two balls are drawn at random <strong>without replacement</strong>.</p><p>Find the probability that <strong>both balls are red</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>
  answer_t: {{fracStr(r*(r-1),(r+b)*(r+b-1))}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>A bag contains <strong>5 red</strong> balls and <strong>5 blue</strong> balls.</p><p>Two balls are drawn at random <strong>without replacement</strong>.</p><p>Find the probability that <strong>both balls are red</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>"  → A="2/9"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>A bag contains <strong>2 red</strong> balls and <strong>4 blue</strong> balls.</p><p>Two balls are drawn at random <strong>without replacement</strong>.</p><p>Find the probability that <strong>both balls are red</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>"  → A="1/15"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>A bag contains <strong>4 red</strong> balls and <strong>4 blue</strong> balls.</p><p>Two balls are drawn at random <strong>without replacement</strong>.</p><p>Find the probability that <strong>both balls are red</strong>. Give your answer as a fraction.</p>"  → A="3/14"
   trap 1: {{fracStr(r*r,(r+b)*(r+b))}}
   trap 2: {{fracStr(r,r+b)}}
   expl_t: P(1st ball red) = {{frac(r, r+b)}}<br>After removing one red ball: {{r-1}} red balls remain out of {{r+b-1}} total.<br>P(2nd ball red | 1st was red) = {{frac(r-1, r+b-1)}}<br>P(both red) = {{frac(r, r+b)}} × {{frac(r-1, r+b-1)}} = {{frac(r*(r-1), (r+b)*(r+b-1))}}

— Mutually Exclusive Events  [LIVE]  diff 3  type:numeric  tol:0.005
  id: 7168c1d6-e36e-4f79-bead-c74bb24ad267
  template: <p>P(A) = <strong>{{a / 10}}</strong> and P(B) = <strong>{{b / 10}}</strong>.</p><p>Events A and B are mutually exclusive.</p><p>Find P(A or B).</p>
  answer_t: {{(a + b) / 10}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>P(A) = <strong>0.1</strong> and P(B) = <strong>0.1</strong>.</p><p>Events A and B are mutually exclusive.</p><p>Find P(A or B).</p>"  → A="0.2"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>P(A) = <strong>0.1</strong> and P(B) = <strong>0.1</strong>.</p><p>Events A and B are mutually exclusive.</p><p>Find P(A or B).</p>"  → A="0.2"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>P(A) = <strong>0.3</strong> and P(B) = <strong>0.1</strong>.</p><p>Events A and B are mutually exclusive.</p><p>Find P(A or B).</p>"  → A="0.4"
   trap 1: {{round((a / 10) * (b / 10), 4)}}
   trap 2: {{round((a + b) / 10 - (a / 10) * (b / 10), 4)}}
   expl_t: Mutually exclusive events cannot both occur, so P(A and B) = 0.<br>P(A or B) = P(A) + P(B) = {{a / 10}} + {{b / 10}} = {{(a + b) / 10}}.

   trap 2: {{round((a + b + c + d + e) / 5, 1)}}

— Angles in Polygons  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: b5edccf8-9acb-4bd7-b45d-ff94000329f1
  template: <p>Find the <strong>sum of interior angles</strong> of a <strong>{{n}}-sided polygon</strong>.</p>
  answer_t: {{(n - 2) * 180}}

   trap 2: {{n * 90}}
   expl_t: Sum of interior angles of an n-sided polygon = (n − 2) × 180°<br>= ({{n}} − 2) × 180°<br>= {{n - 2}} × 180°<br>= {{(n - 2) * 180}}°

   expl_t: Opposite angles of a cyclic quadrilateral sum to 180°.<br>Angle A + Angle C = 180°<br>Angle C = 180° − {{a}}° = {{180 - a}}°

   trap 2: {{round(Math.sqrt((5*k)*(5*k) + (3*k)*(3*k)), 2)}}
   expl_t: The tangent is perpendicular to the radius at P, so angle OPT = 90°.<br>By Pythagoras: PT² = OT² − OP²<br>= {{5*k}}² − {{3*k}}²<br>= {{25*k*k}} − {{9*k*k}}<br>= {{16*k*k}}<br>PT = √{{16*k*k}} = {{4*k}} cm

— Circumfrence of a Circle  [LIVE]  diff 1  type:numeric  tol:0.5
  id: 1c438666-4afa-4ceb-8c1d-219a6c0b6d83
  template: <p>Find the circumference of a circle with radius <strong>{{r}} cm</strong>.</p><p>Give your answer to 2 decimal places.</p>
  answer_t: {{round(2 * Math.PI * r, 2)}} cm
   · sample 1: Q="<p>Find the circumference of a circle with radius <strong>7 cm</strong>.</p><p>Give your answer to 2 decimal places.</p>"  → A="43.98 cm"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>Find the circumference of a circle with radius <strong>9 cm</strong>.</p><p>Give your answer to 2 decimal places.</p>"  → A="56.55 cm"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>Find the circumference of a circle with radius <strong>9 cm</strong>.</p><p>Give your answer to 2 decimal places.</p>"  → A="56.55 cm"
   trap 1: {{round(Math.PI * r * r, 2)}}
   trap 2: {{round(Math.PI * r, 2)}}
   trap 3: {{round(2 * Math.PI * 2 * r, 2)}}
   expl_t: Circumference = 2πr<br>= 2 × π × {{r}}<br>≈ {{round(2 * Math.PI * r, 2)}} cm

— Exterior Angles  [LIVE]  diff 2  type:numeric  tol:0
  id: c0c02057-d8d1-43d1-adf0-4ecc5ee570cf
  template: <p>A <strong>regular polygon</strong> has <strong>{{n}} sides</strong>.</p>
<p>Find the size of each exterior angle.</p>
  answer_t: {{360 / n}}
   · sample 1: Q="<p>A <strong>regular polygon</strong> has <strong>12 sides</strong>.</p> <p>Find the size of each exterior angle.</p>"  → A="30"
   · sample 2: Q="<p>A <strong>regular polygon</strong> has <strong>12 sides</strong>.</p> <p>Find the size of each exterior angle.</p>"  → A="30"
   · sample 3: Q="<p>A <strong>regular polygon</strong> has <strong>3 sides</strong>.</p> <p>Find the size of each exterior angle.</p>"  → A="120"
   trap 1: {{(n - 2) * 180 / n}}
   trap 2: {{180 - 360 / n}}
   expl_t: Exterior angle of a regular polygon = 360° ÷ n<br>
= 360° ÷ {{n}}<br>
= {{360 / n}}°
